=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given sequences of positive integers of length N: A=(A_1,A_2,\ldots,A_N) and B=(B_1,B_2,\ldots,B_N).
You are given Q queries to process in order. The i-th query is explained below.

- You are given positive integers l_i,r_i,L_i,R_i. Print Yes if it is possible to rearrange the subsequence (A_{l_i},A_{l_i+1},\ldots,A_{r_i}) to match the subsequence (B_{L_i},B_{L_i+1},\ldots,B_{R_i}), and No otherwise.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N Q
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N
B_1 B_2 \ldots B_N
l_1 r_1 L_1 R_1
l_2 r_2 L_2 R_2
\vdots
l_Q r_Q L_Q R_Q

Output

Print Q lines. The i-th line should contain the answer to the i-th query.

Constraints


-  1\leq N,Q\leq 2\times 10^5
-  1\leq A_i,B_i\leq N
-  1\leq l_i \leq r_i\leq N
-  1\leq L_i \leq R_i\leq N
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5 4
1 2 3 2 4
2 3 1 4 2
1 3 1 3
1 2 3 5
1 4 2 5
1 5 1 5

Sample Output 1

Yes
No
No
Yes


- For the 1st query, it is possible to rearrange (1,2,3) to match (2,3,1). Hence, we print Yes.
- For the 2nd query, it is impossible to rearrange (1,2) in any way to match (1,4,2). Hence, we print No.
- For the 3rd query, it is impossible to rearrange (1,2,3,2) in any way to match (3,1,4,2). Hence, we print No.
- For the 4th query, it is possible to rearrange (1,2,3,2,4) to match (2,3,1,4,2). Hence, we print Yes.

Sample Input 2

4 4
4 4 4 4
4 4 4 4
1 2 2 3
3 3 1 1
1 3 1 4
1 4 2 3

Sample Output 2

Yes
Yes
No
No

Use the following starter code to write the final solution.
```python

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Let's draft code:

```
import sys, random

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    it = iter(data)
    N = next(it); Q = next(it)
    A = [next(it) for _ in range(N)]
    B = [next(it) for _ in range(N)]
    MOD = (1 << 61) - 1
    # Generate random hash for values 1..N
    rng = random.Random()  # seeded from system
    # Or rng = random.Random(123456789) for deterministic? 
    h = [0]*(N+1)
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        h[i] = rng.randrange(MOD)
    prefA = [0]*(N+1)
    prefB = [0]*(N+1)
    for i, x in enumerate(A, 1):
        prefA[i] = (prefA[i-1] + h[x]) % MOD
    for i, x in enumerate(B, 1):
        prefB[i] = (prefB[i-1] + h[x]) % MOD
    out = []
    for _ in range(Q):
        l = next(it); r = next(it); L = next(it); R = next(it)
        if r - l != R - L:
            out.append("No")
        else:
            if (prefA[r] - prefA[l-1]) % MOD == (prefB[R] - prefB[L-1]) % MOD:
                out.append("Yes")
            else:
                out.append("No")
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

We can parse data list, generate h, then build prefA from data positions 2..2+N-1, prefB next. We don't need A,B lists. Use data list and index. That saves A,B memory. Code:
```
data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
N,Q = data[0], data[1]
MOD=...
h=[0]*(N+1)
for i in range(1,N+1): h[i]=rng.randrange(MOD)
prefA=[0]*(N+1)
prefB=[0]*(N+1)
idx=2
for i in range(1,N+1):
    prefA[i]=(prefA[i-1]+h[data[idx]])%MOD; idx+=1
for i in range(1,N+1):
    prefB[i]=(prefB[i-1]+h[data[idx]])%MOD; idx+=1
out=[]

    l=data[idx]; r=data[idx+1]; L=data[idx+2]; R=data[idx+3]; idx+=4
...
```

Let's optimize code slightly:
```
import sys, random

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]; Q = data[1]
    MOD = (1 << 61) - 1
    rng = random.Random()
    rand = rng.randrange
    h = [0] * (N + 1)
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        h[i] = rand(MOD)

    prefA = [0] * (N + 1)
    prefB = [0] * (N + 1)

    idx = 2
    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s = (s + h[data[idx]]) % MOD
        prefA[i] = s
        idx += 1
    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s = (s + h[data[idx]]) % MOD
        prefB[i] = s
        idx += 1

    out = []
    append = out.append
    for _ in range(Q):
        l = data[idx]; r = data[idx+1]; L = data[idx+2]; R = data[idx+3]; idx += 4
        if r - l != R - L:
            append("No")
        else:
            if (prefA[r] - prefA[l-1]) % MOD == (prefB[R] - prefB[L-1]) % MOD:
                append("Yes")
            else:
                append("No")
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))
```

Now, code with data list:
```
def main():
    import sys, random
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]; Q = data[1]
    MOD = (1 << 61) - 1
    rng = random.Random()
    rand = rng.randrange
    h = [0] * (N + 1)
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        h[i] = rand(MOD)
    prefA = [0] * (N + 1)
    prefB = [0] * (N + 1)
    idx = 2
    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s += h[data[idx]]
        if s >= MOD:
            s -= MOD
        prefA[i] = s
        idx += 1
    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s += h[data[idx]]
        if s >= MOD:
            s -= MOD
        prefB[i] = s
        idx += 1
    out = []
    append = out.append
    for _ in range(Q):
        l = data[idx]; r = data[idx+1]; L = data[idx+2]; R = data[idx+3]
        idx += 4
        if r - l != R - L:
            append("No")
            continue
        sa = prefA[r] - prefA[l-1]
        if sa < 0:
            sa += MOD
        sb = prefB[R] - prefB[L-1]
        if sb < 0:
            sb += MOD
        append("Yes" if sa == sb else "No")
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))
```
Need if `data` is empty? no.

Potential issue: `rand = rng.randrange`; `rand(MOD)` uses method bound to rng. Good.

Let's maybe write final code:

```
import sys
from random import Random

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]
    Q = data[1]
    MOD = (1 << 61) - 1

    rng = Random()
    rand = rng.randrange
    h = [0] * (N + 1)
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        h[i] = rand(MOD)

    prefA = [0] * (N + 1)
    prefB = [0] * (N + 1)

    idx = 2
    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s += h[data[idx]]
        if s >= MOD:
            s -= MOD
        prefA[i] = s
        idx += 1

    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s += h[data[idx]]
        if s >= MOD:
            s -= MOD
        prefB[i] = s
        idx += 1

    del h

    out = []
    append = out.append
    for _ in range(Q):
        l = data[idx]
        r = data[idx + 1]
        L = data[idx + 2]
        R = data[idx + 3]
        idx += 4

        if r - l != R - L:
            append("No")
            continue

        sa = prefA[r] - prefA[l - 1]
        if sa < 0:
            sa += MOD
        sb = prefB[R] - prefB[L - 1]
        if sb < 0:
            sb += MOD

        append("Yes" if sa == sb else "No")

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Let's prove: sa = a - b, with 0 <= a,b < M. If a < b, then sa = -(b-a), where b-a in [1, M-1]. So sa in [-(M-1), -1]. Add M => [1, M-1]. Good. If a >= b, sa in [0, M-1]. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is a possible issue with "s += h[data[idx]]; if s >= MOD: s -= MOD" if s+h can be exactly 2MOD-2 and subtract to MOD-2; yes. If s+h = 2MOD-1? impossible because max h = MOD-1, max s = MOD-1, sum = 2MOD-2. Good.

Now, let's maybe think about if there is a possible issue with "randrange(MOD)" returning values in [0, MOD-1], but if `MOD` is 2^61-1, `MOD-1` is 2^61-2. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is a possible issue with "data" list and "idx" if `N` and `Q` are read from data but data list is empty; no.

Now, let's think about if there is a possible issue with "sys.stdout.write" if out list is large and join memory; okay.

Now, let's maybe think about if there is a possible issue with "random" hash and "mod prime" if h values are generated by `Random()` but the seed is from system, and if the system random source is deterministic in some judge? no.

Now, let's consider if there is a possible issue with "MOD" being prime but if h values are random in [0,M), and if the difference vector c has sum 0 but all coefficients c_v are multiples of M? impossible due to bound. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is a possible issue with "length check" if lengths equal but hash sums equal and actual multiset equal; yes.

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys
from random import Random

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]
    Q = data[1]

    MOD = (1 << 61) - 1

    rng = Random()
    rand = rng.randrange

    h = [0] * (N + 1)
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        h[i] = rand(MOD)

    prefA = [0] * (N + 1)
    prefB = [0] * (N + 1)

    idx = 2
    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s += h[data[idx]]
        if s >= MOD:
            s -= MOD
        prefA[i] = s
        idx += 1

    s = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        s += h[data[idx]]
        if s >= MOD:
            s -= MOD
        prefB[i] = s
        idx += 1

    out = []
    append = out.append

    for _ in range(Q):
        l = data[idx]
        r = data[idx + 1]
        L = data[idx + 2]
        R = data[idx + 3]
        idx += 4

        if r - l != R - L:
            append("No")
            continue

        sa = prefA[r] - prefA[l - 1]
        if sa < 0:
            sa += MOD

        sb = prefB[R] - prefB[L - 1]
        if sb < 0:
            sb += MOD

        append("Yes" if sa == sb else "No")

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(out))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
